Conclusion: 韵律ABX为低资源场景下评估语音模型的韵律感知能力提供了可行方案，且无需大规模标注数据即可实现可靠评估。

Abstract: Speech representations from self-supervised speech models (S3Ms) are known to be sensitive to phonemic contrasts, but their sensitivity to prosodic contrasts has not been directly measured. The ABX discrimination task has been used to measure phonemic contrast in S3M representations via minimal pairs. We introduce prosodic ABX, an extension of this framework to evaluate prosodic contrast with only a handful of examples and no explicit labels. Also, we build and release a dataset of English and Japanese minimal pairs and use it along with a Mandarin dataset to evaluate contrast in English stress, Japanese pitch accent, and Mandarin tone. Finally, we show that model and layer rankings are often preserved across several experimental conditions, making it practical for low-resource settings.

</details>


### [42] [Reliable Control-Point Selection for Steering Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02113)
*Haomin Zhuang,Hojun Yoo,Xiaonan Luo,Kehan Guo,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型中自反射等内在推理行为的控制问题，指出当前基于关键词匹配的检测方法存在严重缺陷——93.3%的检测边界在重新生成时无法复现目标行为。基于此，作者提出稳定性过滤与内容子空间投影相结合的方法，在MATH-500上达到0.784准确率（+5.0），且 steering 向量可在同架构模型间迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多重要推理行为（如自反思）自发涌现，无法通过提示控制。当前方法依赖关键词匹配检测链式思维中的行为边界，但假设每个检测到的边界都编码真实的 behavioral signal，这可能导致 steering 向量质量下降。

Method: 1) 建立概率模型，将内在推理行为形式化为具有上下文相关触发概率的随机事件；2) 开发稳定性过滤方法，仅保留模型能稳定复现目标行为的边界；3) 采用内容子空间投影去除残留的问题特定噪声。

Result: 在541个关键词检测边界中，93.3%具有行为不稳定性。MATH-500准确率达0.784（较最强基线提升5.0）。Steering向量可跨模型迁移：Nemotron-Research-Reasoning-1.5B提升5.0，DeepScaleR-1.5B-Preview提升6.0。

Conclusion: 关键词匹配检测的边界大多不稳定，会稀释 steering 信号。稳定性过滤与内容子空间投影是提升 steering 向量质量与迁移性的有效策略。

Abstract: Steering vectors offer a training-free mechanism for controlling reasoning behaviors in large language models, but constructing effective vectors requires identifying genuine behavioral signals in the model's hidden states. For behaviors that can be toggled via prompts, this is straightforward. However, many reasoning behaviors -- such as self-reflection -- emerge spontaneously and resist prompt-level control. Current methods detect these behaviors through keyword matching in chain-of-thought traces, implicitly assuming that every detected boundary encodes a genuine behavioral signal. We show that this assumption is overwhelmingly wrong: across 541 keyword-detected boundaries, 93.3\% are behaviorally unstable, failing to reproduce the detected behavior under re-generation from the same prefix. We develop a probabilistic model that formalizes intrinsic reasoning behaviors as stochastic events with context-dependent trigger probabilities, and show that unstable boundaries dilute the steering signal. Guided by this analysis, we propose stability filtering, which retains only boundaries where the model consistently reproduces the target behavior. Combined with a content-subspace projection that removes residual question-specific noise, our method achieves 0.784 accuracy on MATH-500 (+5.0 over the strongest baseline). The resulting steering vectors transfer across models in the same architecture family without re-extraction, improving Nemotron-Research-Reasoning-1.5B (+5.0) and DeepScaleR-1.5B-Preview (+6.0). Code is available at https://github.com/zhmzm/stability-steering.

</details>


### [43] [GaelEval: Benchmarking LLM Performance for Scottish Gaelic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02135)
*Peter Devine,William Lamb,Beatrice Alex,Ignatius Ezeani,Dawn Knight,Mícheál J. Ó Meachair,Paul Rayson,Martin Wynne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GaelEval是首个针对苏格兰盖尔语的多维评估基准，包含形态句法选择题、文化翻译基准和文化知识问答三个组件。研究对19个大语言模型进行评估，对照30名母语者。发现Gemini 3 Pro Preview在语言任务上超越人类基准，专有模型优于开源模型，盖尔语提示带来稳定增益，但文化任务得分存在虚高问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型在非官方支持的语言上存在"暗能力"，但这些语言（尤其是形态句法丰富的少数族裔语言）的性能评测不足且不均匀。现有翻译基准无法捕捉结构性能力，因此需要针对苏格兰盖尔语这样形态复杂的语言建立更全面的评估体系。

Method: 构建GaelEval基准，包括：(i)专家编写的形态句法多选问答任务；(ii)文化锚定的翻译基准；(iii)大规模文化知识问答任务。共评估19个LLM模型，以30名盖尔语母语者作为人类基线进行对比。

Result: Gemini 3 Pro Preview在语言任务上达83.3%准确率，超越人类基线(78.1%)。专有模型持续优于开源权重系统，使用盖尔语提示带来+2.4%的稳定增益。文化任务上领先模型准确率超90%，但多数系统在使用盖尔语提示时表现下降，且绝对分数相对人工基准存在虚高。

Conclusion: 前沿模型在盖尔语语法多个维度上已达到超人类表现，验证了盖尔语提示的有效性，并揭示了专有模型相对于开源模型的持续性能差距。文化任务评估存在分数膨胀问题，需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models (LLMs) often exhibit emergent 'shadow' capabilities in languages without official support, yet their performance on these languages remains uneven and under-measured. This is particularly acute for morphosyntactically rich minority languages such as Scottish Gaelic, where translation benchmarks fail to capture structural competence. We introduce GaelEval, the first multi-dimensional benchmark for Gaelic, comprising: (i) an expert-authored morphosyntactic MCQA task; (ii) a culturally grounded translation benchmark and (iii) a large-scale cultural knowledge Q&A task. Evaluating 19 LLMs against a fluent-speaker human baseline ($n=30$), we find that Gemini 3 Pro Preview achieves $83.3\%$ accuracy on the linguistic task, surpassing the human baseline ($78.1\%$). Proprietary models consistently outperform open-weight systems, and in-language (Gaelic) prompting yields a small but stable advantage (+$2.4\%$). On the cultural task, leading models exceed $90\%$ accuracy, though most systems perform worse under Gaelic prompting and absolute scores are inflated relative to the manual benchmark. Overall, GaelEval reveals that frontier models achieve above-human performance on several dimensions of Gaelic grammar, demonstrates the effect of Gaelic prompting and shows a consistent performance gap favouring proprietary over open-weight models.

</details>


### [44] [Brief Is Better: Non-Monotonic Chain-of-Thought Budget Effects in Function-Calling Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02155)
*Xuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: How much should a language agent think before taking action? Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is widely assumed to improve agent performance, but the relationship between reasoning length and accuracy in structured tool-use settings remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of CoT budget effects on function-calling agents, sweeping six token budgets (0--512) across 200 tasks from the Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard v3 Multiple benchmark. Our central finding is a striking non-monotonic pattern on Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct: brief reasoning (32 tokens) dramatically improves accuracy by 45% relative over direct answers, from 44.0% to 64.0%, while extended reasoning (256 tokens) degrades performance well below the no-CoT baseline, to 25.0% (McNemar p < 0.001). A three-way error decomposition reveals the mechanism. At d = 0, 30.5% of tasks fail because the model selects the wrong function from the candidate set; brief CoT reduces this to 1.5%, effectively acting as a function-routing step, while long CoT reverses the gain, yielding 28.0% wrong selections and 18.0% hallucinated functions at d = 256. Oracle analysis shows that 88.6% of solvable tasks require at most 32 reasoning tokens, with an average of 27.6 tokens, and a finer-grained sweep indicates that the true optimum lies at 8--16 tokens. Motivated by this routing effect, we propose Function-Routing CoT (FR-CoT), a structured brief-CoT method that templates the reasoning phase as "Function: [name] / Key args: [...]," forcing commitment to a valid function name at the start of reasoning. FR-CoT achieves accuracy statistically equivalent to free-form d = 32 CoT while reducing function hallucination to 0.0%, providing a structural reliability guarantee without budget tuning.

</details>


### [45] [Do Lexical and Contextual Coreference Resolution Systems Degrade Differently under Mention Noise? An Empirical Study on Scientific Software Mentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02171)
*Atilla Kaan Alkan,Felix Grezes,Jennifer Lynn Bartlett,Anna Kelbert,Kelly Lockhart,Alberto Accomazzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SOMD 2026跨文档软件提及共指消解任务的参赛系统，在三个子任务中均排名第二。对比了两种无需微调的方法：基于字符串相似度的模糊匹配(FM)和结合提及级与文档级嵌入的上下文感知表示(CAR)，两者均达到0.94-0.96的CoNLL F1分数。CAR相比FM在测试集上稳定高出1分。噪声注入实验揭示了互补的失效模式：边界噪声下CAR更鲁棒(损失0.07 vs 0.20)，而提及替换下FM更稳定。CAR具有线性扩展优势，适合大规模场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨文档软件提及共指消解是一个尚未被充分探索的任务，需要识别跨越不同文档引用同一软件实体的提及对。当前方法多依赖复杂语义推理，但软件名称具有较高的表面规律性，可能不需要复杂模型。

Method: 对比两种无需微调的轻量级方法：1) 模糊匹配(FM)：基于词汇字符串相似度；2) 上下文感知表示(CAR)：结合提及级嵌入和文档级嵌入生成联合表示。通过受控噪声注入实验评估系统在边界噪声和提及替换场景下的鲁棒性。

Result: 两种方法均取得竞争力的性能(CoNLL F1: 0.94-0.96)，CAR在所有子任务上稳定优于FM约1个百分点。噪声实验显示：边界噪声下CAR更鲁棒(F1损失0.07 vs 0.20)；提及替换下FM更稳定。扩展性方面，FM随语料规模超线性增长，CAR近似线性增长。

Conclusion: 系统选择应根据上游提及检测器的噪声特征和目标语料规模来决定：上游检测器边界噪声严重时选择CAR，提及替换频繁时选择FM，大规模场景下优先选择CAR以获得线性扩展性。研究为该任务提供了重要的基准和方法论指导。

Abstract: We present our participation in the SOMD 2026 shared task on cross-document software mention coreference resolution, where our systems ranked second across all three subtasks. We compare two fine-tuning-free approaches: Fuzzy Matching (FM), a lexical string-similarity method, and Context Aware Representations (CAR), which combines mention-level and document-level embeddings. Both achieve competitive performance across all subtasks (CoNLL F1 of 0.94-0.96), with CAR consistently outperforming FM by 1 point on the official test set, consistent with the high surface regularity of software names, which reduces the need for complex semantic reasoning. A controlled noise-injection study reveals complementary failure modes: as boundary noise increases, CAR loses only 0.07 F1 points from clean to fully corrupted input, compared to 0.20 for FM, whereas under mention substitution, FM degrades more gracefully (0.52 vs. 0.63). Our inference-time analysis shows that FM scales superlinearly with corpus size, whereas CAR scales approximately linearly, making CAR the more efficient choice at large scale. These findings suggest that system selection should be informed by both the noise profile of the upstream mention detector and the scale of the target corpus. We release our code to support future work on this underexplored task.

</details>


### [46] [Adam's Law: Textual Frequency Law on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02176)
*Hongyuan Adam Lu,Z. L.,Victor Wei,Zefan Zhang,Zhao Hong,Qiqi Xiang,Bowen Cao,Wai Lam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出文本频率定律(TFL)框架，探索文本频率对大语言模型(LLM)的影响。框架包含三个组件：1) 使用在线资源估计句子级频率，通过输入改写器将输入转换为更高频表达；2) 文本频率蒸馏(TFD)通过故事补全任务调整频率估计；3) 课程文本频率训练(CTFT)按频率递增顺序微调LLM。在数学推理、机器翻译、常识推理和工具调用任务上的实验验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本频率已被验证与人类阅读认知相关，但其在LLM中的作用研究不足。多数LLM训练数据封闭，难以直接分析高频文本的影响。因此需要探索一种可操作的方法来研究和利用文本频率提升LLM性能。

Method: 1) 提出文本频率定律(TFL)，假设高频文本更适合LLM提示和微调；2) 利用在线资源估计句子级频率，使用输入改写器(paraphraser)将输入转换为更频繁的表达；3) 提出文本频率蒸馏(TFD)，通过查询LLM进行故事补全扩展句子，修正初始频率估计；4) 提出课程文本频率训练(CTFT)，按频率递增顺序微调LLM。在自建数据集TFPD上开展实验。

Result: 实验在数学推理、机器翻译、常识推理和智能体工具调用四项任务上进行，结果验证了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 文本频率是影响LLM性能的一个重要但被忽视的因素。TFL框架通过频率估计、频率引导改写和课程微调三个组件，有效利用文本频率提升LLM在多种任务上的表现。

Abstract: While textual frequency has been validated as relevant to human cognition in reading speed, its relatedness to Large Language Models (LLMs) is seldom studied. We propose a novel research direction in terms of textual data frequency, which is an understudied topic, to the best of our knowledge. Our framework is composed of three units. First, this paper proposes Textual Frequency Law (TFL), which indicates that frequent textual data should be preferred for LLMs for both prompting and fine-tuning. Since many LLMs are closed-source in their training data, we propose using online resources to estimate the sentence-level frequency. We then utilize an input paraphraser to paraphrase the input into a more frequent textual expression. Next, we propose Textual Frequency Distillation (TFD) by querying LLMs to conduct story completion by further extending the sentences in the datasets, and the resulting corpora are used to adjust the initial estimation. Finally, we propose Curriculum Textual Frequency Training (CTFT) that fine-tunes LLMs in an increasing order of sentence-level frequency. Experiments are conducted on our curated dataset Textual Frequency Paired Dataset (TFPD) on math reasoning, machine translation, commonsense reasoning and agentic tool calling. Results show the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [47] [Neuro-RIT: Neuron-Guided Instruction Tuning for Robust Retrieval-Augmented Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02194)
*Jaemin Kim,Jae O Lee,Sumyeong Ahn,Seo Yeon Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Neuro-RIT通过神经元级别的精准对齐而非粗粒度参数更新，增强检索增强语言模型对噪声上下文的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RALM鲁棒性增强方法采用粗粒度的层或模块级参数更新，忽视了LLM固有的神经元级稀疏性。

Method: 1. 基于归因的神经元挖掘，显式分离处理相关与不相关上下文的神经元；2. 两阶段指令微调策略：功能性地停用仅与不相关上下文相关的神经元以抑制噪声，同时优化目标层进行证据蒸馏。

Result: 在多个QA基准数据集上持续优于强基线和鲁棒性增强方法。

Conclusion: 从密集适配范式转向精准驱动的神经元对齐是提升RALM鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Language Models (RALMs) have demonstrated significant potential in knowledge-intensive tasks; however, they remain vulnerable to performance degradation when presented with irrelevant or noisy retrieved contexts. Existing approaches to enhance robustness typically operate via coarse-grained parameter updates at the layer or module level, often overlooking the inherent neuron-level sparsity of Large Language Models (LLMs). To address this limitation, we propose Neuro-RIT (Neuron-guided Robust Instruction Tuning), a novel framework that shifts the paradigm from dense adaptation to precision-driven neuron alignment. Our method explicitly disentangles neurons that are responsible for processing relevant versus irrelevant contexts using attribution-based neuron mining. Subsequently, we introduce a two-stage instruction tuning strategy that enforces a dual capability for noise robustness: achieving direct noise suppression by functionally deactivating neurons exclusive to irrelevant contexts, while simultaneously optimizing targeted layers for evidence distillation. Extensive experiments across diverse QA benchmarks demonstrate that Neuro-RIT consistently outperforms strong baselines and robustness-enhancing methods.

</details>


### [48] [Towards Position-Robust Talent Recommendation via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02200)
*Silin Du,Hongyan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: L3TR是一种基于LLM的列表式人才推荐框架，通过块注意力机制和局部位置编码解决位置偏差问题，采用ID采样方法弥合训练与推理阶段的不一致，实验表明该方法优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的人才推荐系统多采用逐个(pointwise)范式，需重复处理文本且无法捕捉候选人之间的关系，导致Token消耗高、推荐效果欠佳。此外，LLM在多选问答和多文档处理中存在位置偏差和"中间丢失"问题。

Method: 提出L3TR框架，采用隐式策略利用LLM潜在输出进行推荐。核心方法包括：(1)块注意力机制和局部位置编码，增强文档间处理并缓解位置偏差和并发Token偏差；(2)ID采样方法解决训练与推理阶段候选集大小不一致问题；(3)无训练去偏方法。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的广泛实验验证了L3TR的有效性，相较于现有基线方法取得一致提升。

Conclusion: L3TR通过列表式处理范式和创新的注意力机制，成功降低了Token消耗，同时有效缓解了LLM的位置偏差问题，为基于LLM的人才推荐提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Talent recruitment is a critical, yet costly process for many industries, with high recruitment costs and long hiring cycles. Existing talent recommendation systems increasingly adopt large language models (LLMs) due to their remarkable language understanding capabilities. However, most prior approaches follow a pointwise paradigm, which requires LLMs to repeatedly process some text and fails to capture the relationships among candidates in the list, resulting in higher token consumption and suboptimal recommendations. Besides, LLMs exhibit position bias and the lost-in-the-middle issue when answering multiple-choice questions and processing multiple long documents. To address these issues, we introduce an implicit strategy to utilize LLM's potential output for the recommendation task and propose L3TR, a novel framework for listwise talent recommendation with LLMs. In this framework, we propose a block attention mechanism and a local positional encoding method to enhance inter-document processing and mitigate the position bias and concurrent token bias issue. We also introduce an ID sampling method for resolving the inconsistency between candidate set sizes in the training phase and the inference phase. We design evaluation methods to detect position bias and token bias and training-free debiasing methods. Extensive experiments on two real-world datasets validated the effectiveness of L3TR, showing consistent improvements over existing baselines.

</details>


### [49] [CV-18 NER: Augmented Common Voice for Named Entity Recognition from Arabic Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02209)
*Youssef Saidi,Haroun Elleuch,Fethi Bougares*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首个针对阿拉伯语语音的命名实体识别（NER）公开数据集CV-18 NER，结合Wojood实体模式（21类实体），系统对比了流水线系统（ASR+文本NER）与端到端模型（Whisper/AraBEST-RQ），发现端到端方法显著优于流水线方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语因其形态复杂性、缺乏短元音标注及标注资源匮乏，语音NER任务长期缺乏研究。该研究旨在填补这一空白，首次为阿拉伯语语音NER提供公开基准数据集和方法对比。

Method: 基于Arabic Common Voice 18语料库人工标注NER标签；对比测试流水线系统（ASR + 文本NER串联）与基于Whisper和AraBEST-RQ的端到端模型；评估指标包括CoER和CVER。

Result: 端到端模型显著优于最佳流水线配置：AraBEST-RQ 300M达37.0% CoER，Whisper-medium达38.0% CVER。阿拉伯语自监督预训练提升ASR性能；多语言弱监督更有效迁移至语音-实体联合学习；低资源场景下大模型适配难度更高。

Conclusion: 端到端方法是阿拉伯语语音NER的最优方案；多语言弱监督信号有助于联合学习任务；模型规模与低资源适配性存在权衡取舍。数据集和模型已公开发布于HuggingFace平台。

Abstract: End-to-end speech Named Entity Recognition (NER) aims to directly extract entities from speech. Prior work has shown that end-to-end (E2E) approaches can outperform cascaded pipelines for English, French, and Chinese, but Arabic remains under-explored due to its morphological complexity, the absence of short vowels, and limited annotated resources. We introduce CV-18 NER, the first publicly available dataset for NER from Arabic speech, created by augmenting the Arabic Common Voice 18 corpus with manual NER annotations following the fine-grained Wojood schema (21 entity types). We benchmark both pipeline systems (ASR + text NER) and E2E models based on Whisper and AraBEST-RQ. E2E systems substantially outperform the best pipeline configuration on the test set, reaching 37.0% CoER (AraBEST-RQ 300M) and 38.0% CVER (Whisper-medium). Further analysis shows that Arabic-specific self-supervised pretraining yields strong ASR performance, while multilingual weak supervision transfers more effectively to joint speech-to-entity learning, and that larger models may be harder to adapt in this low-resource setting. Our dataset and models are publicly released, providing the first open benchmark for end-to-end named entity recognition from Arabic speech https://huggingface.co/datasets/Elyadata/CV18-NER.

</details>


### [50] [No Single Best Model for Diversity: Learning a Router for Sample Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02319)
*Yuhan Liu,Fangyuan Xu,Vishakh Padmakumar,Daphne Ippolito,Eunsol Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究如何从大型语言模型中生成全面多样的回答。提出"多样性覆盖率"指标，评估18个LLM，发现无单一模型在所有开放性提示上均表现最佳，但每个提示都存在一个最优模型。基于此开发了模型路由器，在NB-Wildchat上超越单最佳模型基准（26.3% vs 23.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当面对允许大量有效答案的提示时，现有方法难以全面生成多样化回答。缺乏统一的评估指标和跨模型选择策略。

Method: 提出"多样性覆盖率"（diversity coverage）指标；评估18个LLM的多样性生成能力；开发模型路由器预测每个查询的最佳模型。

Result: 无单一模型在所有开放性提示上占主导，但每个提示都存在显著优于其他模型的最优模型；训练后的路由器在NB-Wildchat上表现优于单最佳模型（26.3% vs 23.8%），并泛化至NB-Curated及不同提示策略。

Conclusion: 该工作为多模型环境下生成全面答案的研究奠定了基础，通过路由器机制可有效整合多个模型的优势，提升答案多样性覆盖率。

Abstract: When posed with prompts that permit a large number of valid answers, comprehensively generating them is the first step towards satisfying a wide range of users. In this paper, we study methods to elicit a comprehensive set of valid responses. To evaluate this, we introduce \textbf{diversity coverage}, a metric that measures the total quality scores assigned to each \textbf{unique} answer in the predicted answer set relative to the best possible answer set with the same number of answers. Using this metric, we evaluate 18 LLMs, finding no single model dominates at generating diverse responses to a wide range of open-ended prompts. Yet, per each prompt, there exists a model that outperforms all other models significantly at generating a diverse answer set. Motivated by this finding, we introduce a router that predicts the best model for each query. On NB-Wildchat, our trained router outperforms the single best model baseline (26.3% vs $23.8%). We further show generalization to an out-of-domain dataset (NB-Curated) as well as different answer-generation prompting strategies. Our work lays foundation for studying generating comprehensive answers when we have access to a suite of models.

</details>


### [51] [Grounded Token Initialization for New Vocabulary in LMs for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02324)
*Daiwei Chen,Zhoutong Fu,Chengming Jiang,Haichao Zhang,Ran Zhou,Tan Wang,Chunnan Yao,Guoyao Li,Rui Cai,Yihan Cao,Ruijie Jiang,Fedor Borisyuk,Jianqiang Shen,Jingwei Wu,Ramya Korlakai Vinayak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究了语言模型扩展新词汇token的标准初始化策略，发现均值初始化会导致token退化为同一子空间，提出语言学锚定的token初始化方法GTI，在多个生成式推荐基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型扩展新词汇token的标准做法是用现有词嵌入的均值进行初始化，然后通过监督微调学习表示。然而，这种策略存在根本性问题：所有新token被压缩到退化的子空间中，抹去了token间的区分性，而后续微调难以完全恢复这一结构。

Method: 论文通过频谱诊断和几何诊断揭示了均值初始化的问题本质。为解决这一问题，提出了"Grounded Token Initialization假说"：在微调前将新token锚定在预训练嵌入空间的语言学有意义位置，使模型能更好地迁移其通用知识。具体实现为GTI方法：仅使用配对的语言学监督信息，在微调前将新token映射到预训练嵌入空间中语义明确的区域。

Result: GTI在多个生成式推荐基准（包括工业规模数据集和公开数据集）的大多数评估设置中，优于均值初始化和现有辅助任务适配方法。

Conclusion: 锚定的嵌入产生更丰富的token间结构，且该结构在微调后得以保留，证实了初始化质量是词汇扩展的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are increasingly extended with new learnable vocabulary tokens for domain-specific tasks, such as Semantic-ID tokens in generative recommendation. The standard practice initializes these new tokens as the mean of existing vocabulary embeddings, then relies on supervised fine-tuning to learn their representations. We present a systematic analysis of this strategy: through spectral and geometric diagnostics, we show that mean initialization collapses all new tokens into a degenerate subspace, erasing inter-token distinctions that subsequent fine-tuning struggles to fully recover. These findings suggest that \emph{token initialization} is a key bottleneck when extending LMs with new vocabularies. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose the \emph{Grounded Token Initialization Hypothesis}: linguistically grounding novel tokens in the pretrained embedding space before fine-tuning better enables the model to leverage its general-purpose knowledge for novel-token domains. We operationalize this hypothesis as GTI (Grounded Token Initialization), a lightweight grounding stage that, prior to fine-tuning, maps new tokens to distinct, semantically meaningful locations in the pretrained embedding space using only paired linguistic supervision. Despite its simplicity, GTI outperforms both mean initialization and existing auxiliary-task adaptation methods in the majority of evaluation settings across multiple generative recommendation benchmarks, including industry-scale and public datasets. Further analyses show that grounded embeddings produce richer inter-token structure that persists through fine-tuning, corroborating the hypothesis that initialization quality is a key bottleneck in vocabulary extension.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [52] [Training-Free Private Synthesis with Validation: A New Frontier for Practical Educational Data Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01821)
*Hibiki Ito,Chia-Yu Hsu,Hiroaki Ogata*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种实用的差分隐私合成数据生成（DP-SDG）两阶段方法，用于教育领域的真实世界数据共享。该方法利用无需训练的LLM进行DP-SDG，并结合按需真实数据验证，解决了教育数据碎片化、小样本高维度等难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育领域的真实世界数据（RWD）共享受隐私约束限制严重。现有DP-SDG方法需要针对每个数据集定制，且深度学习方法对小样本高维数据难以实现，门槛高、实用性差，亟需一种低工程成本的实用解决方案。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：（1）基于LLM的无训练差分隐私合成数据生成，用于共享合成数据；（2）按需真实数据验证，研究人员提交代码进行远程结果验证。该方法适用于无专业DP-SDG知识的数据管理者，可适应多轮合成场景。实验使用三年收集的RWD数据，分别在单次和多轮合成设置下评估，并与深度学习基线方法对比。

Result: LLM-based DP-SDG性能与DL基线相当，但工程成本大幅降低。非DP验证会造成可测量但适度的隐私泄露。案例研究中，研究人员平均仅36%的合成数据发现能在真实数据上验证。

Conclusion: 该方法为教育RWD共享提供了实用方案，同时揭示了风险缓解和认知精确方面的挑战：隐私泄露风险仍存在，且合成数据的验证覆盖率有限。

Abstract: While secondary use of real-world data (RWD) in education offers substantial research opportunities, data sharing is often limited by privacy constraints. Differentially private synthetic data generation (DP-SDG) has emerged as a possible solution. However, educational RWD is fragmented across platforms and institutions and stored in different formats, so DP-SDG must be tailored to each dataset, requiring substantial engineering effort. In addition, such data are often small-sample and high-dimensional, making deep learning (DL)-based methods common but difficult to implement without specialist expertise. In this setting, it is also hard to achieve practically useful downstream utility. As a result, despite its theoretical promise, DP-SDG remains far from a practical solution in education. To address this issue, we propose a more practical two-stage method: (1) training-free, LLM-based DP-SDG is performed for sharing synthetic data and (2) on-demand real-data validation, where researchers submit code for remote validation of results. This simple method is designed for individual data custodians without extensive DP-SDG expertise. It can also be adapted to multi-shot synthesis, where data from different learner cohorts are synthesised regularly. We evaluate this method experimentally in both the one-shot and multi-shot synthesis settings using RWD collected over three years and conduct a case study with real researchers. Results show that LLM-based DP-SDG performs comparably to a DL-based baseline while greatly reducing engineering costs, and that non-DP validation causes measurable but moderate privacy leakage. Nonetheless, in the case study researchers reported that on average only 36% of synthetic findings are validated on real data. Overall, the paper provides a practical method for sharing educational RWD, while highlighting challenges in risk mitigation and epistemic precision.

</details>


### [53] [Teaching Students to Question the Machine: An AI Literacy Intervention Improves Students' Regulation of LLM Use in a Science Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01955)
*O. Clerc,R. Abdelghani,C. Desvaux,E. Poisson,P. Y. Oudeyer,H. Sauzéon*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究探讨了两小时AI素养工作坊对中学生在大语言模型支持的科学探究任务中的影响，发现经过培训的学生的查询重述、追问行为和答案判断准确性均有提升，最终表现优于对照组，表明简短可扩展的AI素养培训能有效改善学生的AI交互策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在校园快速普及引发对学生不加批判地依赖AI输出的担忧。中学生元认知调节能力尚在发展中，容易过度信任AI；且课堂时间和教师培训资源有限，亟需可在真实学校条件下实施的AI素养干预方案。

Method: 采用对照实验设计，116名8-9年级学生（13-15岁）使用生成式AI系统完成六项科学探究任务。实验组在任务前两天参加两小时AI素养工作坊（内容涵盖LLM工作原理与局限性、提示词技巧、响应评估），对照组未接受任何培训。

Result: 培训组学生表现出更少的盲目依赖：更多进行查询重述、追问问题、更准确判断响应正确性，最终表现更佳。GenAI和元认知自我报告分数均未预测表现，说明有效使用生成式AI更多取决于交互调节的显性训练，而非自我报告指标。

Conclusion: 简短、可扩展的AI素养教学可显著改善中学生在学校学习活动中使用生成式AI的方式，为在资源有限的学校环境中开展AI素养教育提供了实证支持。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) in schools raises concerns about students' uncritical reliance on its outputs. Effective use of large language models (LLMs) requires not only technical knowledge but also the ability to monitor, evaluate, and regulate one's interaction with the system, processes closely tied to metacognitive regulation. These skills are still developing in middle school, making students particularly vulnerable to over-trust and premature acceptance of AI outputs. Because classroom time and teacher training resources are constrained, there is a pressing need to develop and evaluate AI literacy interventions that can be implemented under realistic school conditions. We report a controlled classroom study examining whether a two-hour AI literacy workshop improves students' interaction strategies and quality of final answers in LLM-supported science problem solving. A total of 116 students (grades 8-9; ages 13-15) completed six science investigation tasks using a generative AI system. Two days prior, the intervention group attended the workshop, which combined information about how LLMs work and fail with practical guidance on prompting and response evaluation; the control group received no training. Trained students showed less uncritical reliance on the system: they more often reformulated queries, asked follow-up questions, and more accurately judged response correctness, leading to better performance. In contrast, GenAI and metacognitive self-report scores did not predict performance, suggesting that effective use of generative AI depends less on self-reported measures and more on explicit training in interaction regulation. Overall, the results show that brief, scalable AI literacy instruction can meaningfully improve how middle-school students use generative AI in school-like learning activities.

</details>


### [54] [Generative AI Spotlights the Human Core of Data Science: Implications for Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02238)
*Nathan Taback*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 生成式AI已能自动化执行数据科学的常规工作流程（如清洗、可视化、建模等），但人类核心能力（问题构建、因果识别、伦理推理等）依然不可替代。文章基于Donoho的"更大数据科学"框架，分析了GAI对数据科学各分支的影响，提出教育应聚焦于人类核心能力，并利用检索增强生成等技术培养学生的人机协作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI（GAI）能够执行越来越多的常规数据科学工作流程，数据科学教育面临根本性挑战：课程内容需要重新定位，以应对技术变革带来的冲击，明确区分哪些能力应继续由人类掌握，哪些可由机器替代。

Method: 研究采用理论框架分析的方法，将GAI的影响映射到Donoho的"更大数据科学"（GDS）六分框架上，追溯数据科学通过三条脉络的演进：Tukey的数据分析科学愿景、监视资本主义的商业逻辑、以及后续的学术项目设置。

Result: 分析表明：GDS3（数据计算）已被大幅自动化；GDS1（数据收集与探索）和GDS6（数据科学之科学）仍需关键人类参与。人类核心能力包括：问题构建、测量与设计、因果识别、统计与计算推理、伦理与问责、以及意义建构。

Conclusion: 数据科学教育应聚焦于人类核心能力，培养学生在"提示-输出-提示"迭代循环中有效贡献的能力，学习成果与评估应明确考察推理与判断能力，同时强调McLuhan-Culkin洞见——我们塑造工具，工具随后塑造我们。

Abstract: Generative AI (GAI) reveals an irreducible human core at the center of data science: advances in GAI should sharpen, rather than diminish, the focus on human reasoning in data science education. GAI can now execute many routine data science workflows, including cleaning, summarizing, visualizing, modeling, and drafting reports. Yet the competencies that matter most remain irreducibly human: problem formulation, measurement and design, causal identification, statistical and computational reasoning, ethics and accountability, and sensemaking. Drawing on Donoho's Greater Data Science framework, Nolan and Temple Lang's vision of computational literacy, and the McLuhan-Culkin insight that we shape our tools and thereafter our tools shape us, this paper traces the emergence of data science through three converging lineages: Tukey's intellectual vision of data analysis as a science, the commercial logic of surveillance capitalism that created industrial demand for data scientists, and the academic programs that followed. Mapping GAI's impact onto Donoho's six divisions of Greater Data Science shows that computing with data (GDS3) has been substantially automated, while data gathering, preparation, and exploration (GDS1) and science about data science (GDS6) still require essential human input. The educational implication is that data science curricula should focus on this human core while teaching students how to contribute effectively within iterative prompt-output-prompt cycles using retrieval-augmented generation, and that learning outcomes and assessments should explicitly evaluate reasoning and judgment.

</details>

### [55] [EXaCTz: Guaranteed Extremum Graph and Contour Tree Preservation for Distributed- and GPU-Parallel Lossy Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01397)
*Yuxiao Li,Mingze Xia,Xin Liang,Bei Wang,Hanqi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EXaCTz是一种并行算法，通过有界迭代实现失真压缩标量场数据中极值图和轮廓树的保拓扑压缩校正，速度可达单GPU 4.52 GB/s，在128 GPU规模下保持55.6%并行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑保持压缩方法存在三大缺陷：(1) 吞吐量严重失衡——拓扑校正速度仅MB/s级，远落后于GB/s级的压缩速度；(2) 拓扑描述符支持单一；(3) 缺乏理论收敛保证。

Method: EXaCTz提出高性能有界迭代算法，通过对解压数据进行目标性编辑来强制拓扑一致性。核心策略：(1) 强制所有顶点的极值邻域一致；(2) 建立关键点间的全局排序。从而保证关键点分类一致、鞍点-极值连通性及合并/分裂事件的正确性。

Result: 单GPU吞吐量达4.52 GB/s；相比Gorski等人方法提速213x（CPU单核对比）和3285x（GPU版本对比）；分布式环境下可扩展至128 GPU（效率55.6%， naive方法仅6.4%）；512 GB数据集处理时间<48秒，聚合校正吞吐量达32.69 GB/s。

Conclusion: EXaCTz通过理论证明算法收敛性（上界为描述级联效应的脆弱图最长路径），成功解决了拓扑保持压缩的吞吐量差距、多描述符支持和理论收敛三大挑战。

Abstract: This paper introduces EXaCTz, a parallel algorithm that concurrently preserves extremum graphs and contour trees in lossy-compressed scalar field data. While error-bounded lossy compression is essential for large-scale scientific simulations and workflows, existing topology-preserving methods suffer from (1) a significant throughput disparity, where topology correction speeds are on the order of MB/s, lagging orders of magnitude behind compression speeds on the order of GB/s, (2) limited support for diverse topological descriptors, and (3) a lack of theoretical convergence bounds. To address these challenges, EXaCTz introduces a high-performance, bounded-iteration algorithm that enforces topological consistency by deriving targeted edits for decompressed data. Unlike prior methods that rely on explicit topology reconstruction, EXaCTz enforces consistent min/max neighbors of all vertices, along with global ordering among critical points. As such, the algorithm enforces consistent critical-point classification, saddle extremum connectivity, and the preservation of merge/split events. We theoretically prove the convergence of our algorithm, bounded by the longest path in a vulnerability graph that characterizes potential cascading effects during correction. Experiments on real-world datasets show that EXaCTz achieves a single-GPU throughput of up to 4.52 GB/s, outperforming the state-of-the-art contour-tree-preserving method (Gorski et al.) by up to 213x (with a single-core CPU implementation for fair comparison) and 3,285x (with a single-GPU version). In distributed environments, EXaCTz scales to 128 GPUs with 55.6\% efficiency (compared with 6.4\% for a naive parallelization), processing datasets of up to 512 GB in under 48 seconds and achieving an aggregate correction throughput of up to 32.69 GB/s.

</details>


### [56] [ModTrans: Translating Real-world Models for Distributed Training Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01607)
*Yi Lyu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ModTrans，一个支持将任何真实世界模型格式转换为ASTRA-sim模拟器输入格式的翻译器，解决了当前分布式训练模拟器无法导入真实模型的问题，降低了机器学习专家与系统研究人员之间的协作门槛。实验表明ModTrans的开销可以忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前分布式训练模拟器（如ASTRA-sim）不支持导入真实世界开发的模型，导致机器学习研究人员在进行实验时面临物理机器和资源不足的困难，同时也阻碍了ML专家与ML系统研究人员之间的协作。

Method: 开发了ModTrans翻译器，支持任意真实世界模型格式到ASTRA-sim模拟器输入格式的转换，从而消除ML专家与ML系统研究人员之间的技术壁垒。

Result: 实验结果表明ModTrans的转换开销可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: ModTrans成功搭建了真实世界模型与分布式训练模拟器之间的桥梁，使得ML研究人员能够便捷地利用模拟器进行大规模分布式训练实验，无需依赖实际硬件资源。

Abstract: Large-scale distributed training has been a research hot spot in machine learning systems for industry and academia in recent years. However, conducting experiments without physical machines and corresponding resources is difficult. One solution is to leverage distributed training simulators, but current ones like ASTRA-sim do not support importing real-world developed models, which poses challenges for ML researchers seeking to use them. Based on this challenge, we developed ModTrans, a translator supporting format translation from any real-world model to the ASTRA-sim simulator's input, removing the barrier between machine learning experts and machine learning system researchers. The experiment results show that ModTrans's cost is negligible.

</details>


### [57] [DWDP: Distributed Weight Data Parallelism for High-Performance LLM Inference on NVL72](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01621)
*Wanqian Li,Jintao Peng,Zongfei Jing,Tianyu Zhang,Ze Long,Xianjie Qiao,Xiaoming Chen,Dongxu Yang,Kefeng Duan,June Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DWDP提出了一种新的推理并行化策略，通过分布式权重数据并行消除层间跨_rank同步开销，配合权重分割管理和异步预取优化，在GB200 NVL72集群上使用DeepSeek-R1实现了8.8%的TPS/GPU提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理并行化策略依赖层间跨_rank同步，导致端到端性能对负载不均衡敏感，限制了多GPU推理效率的进一步提升。

Method: 提出DWDP（分布式权重数据并行）策略，将MoE权重卸载到各GPU peer端，按需获取缺失的expert；移除跨_rank集合通信；实现权重分割管理和异步远程权重预取两大优化。实现于TensorRT-LLM框架。

Result: 在GB200 NVL72上使用DeepSeek-R1测试，输入序列8K、输出序列1K条件下，20-100 TPS/user服务范围内，TPS/GPU提升8.8%，TPS/user持平。

Conclusion: DWDP通过消除集合同步实现了各GPU独立推进，为MoE模型的多GPU推理提供了高效的并行化方案，兼具数据并行负载均衡优势与权重卸载的内存效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference increasingly depends on multi-GPU execution, yet existing inference parallelization strategies require layer-wise inter-rank synchronization, making end-to-end performance sensitive to workload imbalance. We present DWDP (Distributed Weight Data Parallelism), an inference parallelization strategy that preserves data-parallel execution while offloading MoE weights across peer GPUs and fetching missing experts on demand. By removing collective inter-rank synchronization, DWDP allows each GPU to progress independently. We further address the practical overheads of this design with two optimizations for split-weight management and asynchronous remote-weight prefetch. Implemented in TensorRT-LLM and evaluated with DeepSeek-R1 on GB200 NVL72, DWDP improves end-to-end output TPS/GPU by 8.8% at comparable TPS/user in the 20-100 TPS/user serving range under 8K input sequence length and 1K output sequence length.

</details>


### [58] [Optimization Opportunities for Cloud-Based Data Pipeline Infrastructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01954)
*Johannes Jablonski,Georg-Daniel Schwarz,Philip Heltweg,Dirk Riehle*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对云基础设施中数据管道的优化方法进行了系统性文献综述，归纳了优化目标理论（包括成本最小化、执行时间减少、成本-完工时间权衡），并指出了当前研究的不足与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据管道在云基础设施上的广泛应用，如何在成本、速度和资源利用率之间实现高效优化成为关键挑战。然而，现有研究缺乏对云数据管道优化方法的系统性梳理与整合视角。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，收集并分析了关于云基础设施数据管道性能优化方法的现有研究，构建了优化目标理论框架。

Result: 提出了包含多个维度的优化目标理论（如单云vs多云、批处理vs流处理），并识别出当前研究的主要空白：多租户环境探索不足、工业场景评估缺失等。

Conclusion: 该研究为云数据管道优化提供了系统性的理论框架，同时揭示了未来研究的潜在方向，包括多租户场景优化和工业实践验证。

Abstract: Cloud infrastructure supports the efficient operation of data pipelines regarding requirements like cost, speed, and resource utilization. We present an integrated view of optimization opportunities for cloud-based data pipelines by conducting a systematic review of existing literature on optimization approaches to cloud infrastructure performance for data pipelines. Our study contributes a theory of optimization goals like minimizing cost, reducing execution time, and cost-makespan trade-offs, consisting of dimensions such as single vs. multi-cloud, batch vs. stream processing, etc. We highlight gaps in primary research, including the underexploration of multi-tenant environments and lack of industry evaluation, and suggest directions for future research.

</details>


### [59] [A Practical Two-Stage Framework for GPU Resource and Power Prediction in Heterogeneous HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02158)
*Beste Oztop,Dhruva Kulkarni,Zhengji Zhao,Ayse Kivilcim Coskun,Kadidia Konate*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究针对NERSC Perlmutter超级计算机上的VASP材料科学应用，分析GPU资源利用与功耗，并提出基于Slurm日志和DCGM指标的两阶段GPU功耗与利用率预测框架，实现最高97%的GPU利用率和92%的功耗预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GPU在高性能计算中的广泛使用，高效利用GPU资源和功耗管理变得至关重要。现有调度系统缺乏对应用GPU资源需求的精准预测能力，导致资源分配效率低下和功耗浪费。

Method: 1) 收集Slurm工作负载管理器历史日志和NVIDIA DCGM性能指标；2) 构建两阶段预测框架——第一阶段仅使用Slurm提交特征，第二阶段加入历史GPU性能指标；3) 提取GPU计算和内存活动指标特征进行相关性分析。

Result: 仅使用Slurm提交特征的GPU利用率预测准确率最高达97%；GPU计算和内存活动指标与功耗具有良好相关性，功耗预测准确率最高达92%。

Conclusion: DCGM指标能有效捕获应用特征，基于历史GPU性能指标的预测模型可为HPC系统动态功耗管理提供支持，实现更高效的调度决策和功耗感知系统运行。

Abstract: Efficient utilization of GPU resources and power has become critical with the growing demand for GPUs in high-performance computing (HPC). In this paper, we analyze GPU utilization and GPU memory utilization, as well as the power consumption of the Vienna ab initio Simulation Package (VASP), using the Slurm workload manager historical logs and GPU performance metrics collected by NVIDIA's Data Center GPU Manager (DCGM). VASP is a widely used materials science application on Perlmutter at NERSC, an HPE Cray EX system based on NVIDIA A100 GPUs. Using our insights from the resource utilization analysis of VASP applications, we propose a resource prediction framework to predict the average GPU power, maximum GPU utilization, and maximum GPU memory utilization values of heterogeneous HPC system applications to enable more efficient scheduling decisions and power-aware system operation. Our prediction framework consists of two stages: 1) using only the Slurm accounting logs as training data and 2) augmenting the training data with historical GPU profiling metrics collected with DCGM. The maximum GPU utilization predictions using only the Slurm submission features achieve up to 97% accuracy. Furthermore, features engineered from GPU-compute and memory activity metrics exhibit good correlations with average power utilization, and our runtime power usage prediction experiments result in up to 92% prediction accuracy. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of DCGM metrics in capturing application characteristics and highlight their potential for developing predictive models to support dynamic power management in HPC systems.

</details>


### [60] [What can be computed in average anonymous networks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02192)
*Joel Rybicki,Oleg Verbitsky,Maksim Zhukovskii*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了极弱分布式计算模型（SB和MB模型）下，在随机输入图上能几乎确定计算的问题。在节点匿名、仅支持广播通信的条件下，给出了一个使用O(log n)比特消息的一轮确定型算法，能以高概率在G(n,p)随机图上计算唯一标识符，并由此推导出三角形发现等问题的匿名算法。此外，文章还建立了随机图环境下"Monte Carlo"和"La Vegas"算法的能力层级关系，并证明在G(n,p)上SB模型与LOCAL模型等价的坍缩结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式计算中，现有算法大多依赖节点拥有唯一标识符等强假设。研究的核心动机在于探索在极弱模型（节点匿名、仅广播）下，随机输入图结构本身能否弥补计算能力的不足，从而实现原本需要强假设才能完成的计算任务。

Method: 方法主要包括：（1）借鉴图同构测试中的典范标记（canonical labeling）技术，设计一round O(log n)比特消息的SB算法；（2）利用随机图的高度连通性结构作为"隐式协调机制"，替代全局标识符；（3）对Monte Carlo和Las Vegas变体算法建立复杂度层级关系，通过结构化博弈论证坍缩与分层现象。

Result: 主要成果包括：①对任意ε>0及p∈[n^(ε-1), 1/2]，存在一轮确定型SB算法使用O(log n)比特消息，以高概率在G(n,p)上计算唯一标识符；②可将该算法作为"匿名化"模块，将标准CONGEST/LOCAL模型算法转化为匿名算法；③给出一个O(1/ε)轮匿名算法可在G(n,p)上发现三角形；④证明在随机图上SB模型层次结构发生坍缩，SB模型的力量与LOCAL模型等价。

Conclusion: 结论表明，在随机输入图假设下，极弱分布式模型的计算能力远超其在一般图上的表现。具体而言，随机图的结构特性（如高连通性）能够弥补节点匿名和广播通信的限制，使得原本需要唯一标识符才能完成的任务在匿名条件下也可完成。这一发现揭示了输入分布假设对分布式算法设计的深远影响，并为将现有算法适配至极弱模型提供了通用框架。

Abstract: We study what deterministic distributed algorithms can compute on random input graphs in extremely weak models of distributed computing: all nodes are anonymous, and in each communication round, nodes broadcast a message to all their neighbors, receive a (multi)set of messages from their neighbors, and update their local state. These correspond to the SB and MB models introduced by Hella et al. [PODC 2012] and are strictly weaker than the standard port-numbering PN and LOCAL models.
  We investigate what can be computed almost surely on random input graphs. We give a one-round deterministic SB-algorithm using $O(\log n)$-bit messages that computes unique identifiers with high probability on anonymous networks sampled from $G(n,p)$, where $n^{\varepsilon-1} \le p \le 1/2$ and $\varepsilon>0$ is an arbitrarily small constant. This algorithm is inspired by canonical labeling techniques in graph isomorphism testing and can be used to "anonymize" existing distributed graph algorithms designed for the broadcast CONGEST and LOCAL models. In particular, we give a new anonymous algorithm that finds a triangle in $O(1/\varepsilon)$ rounds on the above input distribution.
  We also investigate computational power of natural analogs of "Monte Carlo" and "Las Vegas" distributed graph algorithms in the random graph setting, and establish some new collapse and hierarchy results. For example, our work shows the collapse of the weak model hierarchy of Hella et al. on $G(n,p)$, as apart from a vanishingly small fraction of input graphs, the SB model is as powerful as LOCAL.

</details>


### [61] [Source Known Identifiers: A Three-Tier Identity System for Distributed Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00151)
*Duran Serkan Kılıç*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SKIDs（源已知标识符），一种三层身份系统，通过64位SKID、128位SKEID和安全SKEID三个层级，在统一框架内同时满足分布式应用标识符的六大需求：存储效率、时序可排序性、起源元数据嵌入、零查找可验证性、外部保密性和跨世纪可寻址性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前分布式应用需要同时满足六大标识符属性：存储效率、时序可排序性、起源元数据嵌入、零查找可验证性、外部保密性和跨世纪可寻址性。但现有方案中没有任何一个能在统一系统内同时提供这六种属性。

Method: 提出三层身份系统：(1) SKID层：64位有符号整数，嵌入时间戳（250毫秒精度）、应用拓扑和实体类型序列计数器，作为数据库主键，支持B树排序；(2) SKEID层：128位UUID兼容值，通过添加实体类型区分符、纪元选择器和BLAKE3 MAC扩展SKID，支持可信环境内零查找验证；(3) 安全SKEID层：使用AES-256对整个SKEID进行单块伪随机置换加密，生成与随机字节不可区分的密文，同时兼容标准UUID数据解析器。三层之间通过确定性双向转换连接。

Result: 在统一系统内同时实现全部六个标识符属性，支持跨信任边界的单一实体身份投射。

Conclusion: SKIDs通过三层架构设计，成功将实体身份投影至不同信任边界，在紧凑存储（8字节主键）的同时兼顾可验证性、安全性和长期可寻址性，为分布式应用提供了一套完整的标识符解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed applications need identifiers that satisfy storage efficiency, chronological sortability, origin metadata embedding, zero-lookup verifiability, confidentiality for external consumers, and multi-century addressability. Based on our literature survey, no existing scheme provides all six of these identifier properties within a unified system.
  This paper introduces Source Known Identifiers (SKIDs), a three-tier identity system that projects a single entity identity across trust boundaries, addressing all six properties. The first tier, Source Known ID (SKID), is a 64-bit signed integer embedding a timestamp with a 250-millisecond precision, application topology, and a per-entity-type sequence counter. It serves as the database primary key, providing compact storage (8 bytes) and natural B-tree ordering for optimized database indexing. The second tier, Source Known Entity ID (SKEID), extends the SKID into a 128-bit Universally Unique Identifier (UUID) compatible value by adding an entity type discriminator, an epoch selector, and a BLAKE3 keyed message authentication code (MAC). SKEIDs enable zero-lookup verification of identifier origin, integrity, and entity type within trusted environments, with a big-endian byte layout that preserves chronological ordering in lexicographic UUID string comparisons. The third tier, Secure SKEID, encrypts the entire SKEID using AES-256 symmetric encryption as a single-block pseudorandom permutation, producing ciphertext indistinguishable from random bytes while remaining compatible with standard UUID data-type parsers in string representation. Deterministic bidirectional transformations connect all three tiers.

</details>

### [62] [The Digital Twin Counterfactual Framework: A Validation Architecture for Simulated Potential Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01325)
*Olav Laudy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出数字孪生反事实框架(DTCF)，通过构建数字孪生模拟器来模拟而非统计估计反事实结果，并建立分层验证体系。该框架将因果量分解为可验证的边际因果量(ATE, CATE, QTE)和依赖copula结构的联合因果量(ITE分布、获益/危害概率等)，并提供边界、敏感性和不确定性量化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断的根本问题在于反事实结果永远无法观测，现有方法都通过假设替代缺失数据。该研究认为现有范式无法产生真正的反事实，因此提出利用数字孪生技术模拟反事实。

Method: 1) 将数字孪生模拟器形式化为潜在结果框架内的随机映射；2) 引入边际保真度、联合保真度、结构保真度的分层孪生保真度假设层级；3) 提出五级验证架构，将不可证伪的主张转化为可证伪的测试；4) 建立因果量的形式化分解，区分边际可验证量和copula依赖量。

Result: 提出三种核心贡献：1) 五级验证架构；2) 因果量的形式化分解（边际验证部分与copula依赖部分）；3) 边界、敏感性和不确定性量化工具，使copula依赖性变得明确。

Conclusion: DTCF并不能解决因果推断的根本问题，但提供了一种框架：使边际因果声称变得可检验，联合因果声称变得明确假设索引化，并形式化表征两者之间的差距。

Abstract: The fundamental problem of causal inference - that the counterfactual outcome for any individual is never observed - has shaped the entire methodology of the field. Every existing approach substitutes assumptions for missing data: ignorability, parallel trends, exclusion restrictions. None produces the counterfactual itself. This paper proposes the Digital Twin Counterfactual Framework (DTCF): rather than estimating the counterfactual statistically, we simulate it using a digital twin and subject the simulation to a hierarchical validation regime. We formalize the digital twin simulator as a stochastic mapping within the potential outcomes framework and introduce a hierarchy of twin fidelity assumptions - from marginal fidelity through joint fidelity to structural fidelity - each unlocking a progressively richer class of estimands. The central contribution is threefold. First, a five-level validation architecture converts the unfalsifiable claim that the simulator produces correct counterfactuals into falsifiable tests against observable data. Second, a formal decomposition separates causal quantities into those that are marginally validated (ATE, CATE, QTE - testable through observable-arm comparison) and those that are copula-dependent (the ITE distribution, probability of benefit/harm, variance of treatment effects - permanently reliant on the unobservable within-individual dependence structure). Third, bounding, sensitivity, and uncertainty quantification tools make the copula dependence explicit. The DTCF does not resolve the fundamental problem of causal inference. What it provides is a framework in which marginal causal claims become increasingly testable, joint causal claims become explicitly assumption-indexed, and the gap between the two is formally characterized.

</details>


### [63] [IDEA2: Expert-in-the-loop competency question elicitation for collaborative ontology engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01344)
*Elliott Watkiss-Leek,Reham Alharbi,Harry Rostron,Andrew Ng,Ewan Johnson,Andrew Mitchell,Terry R. Payne,Valentina Tamma,Jacopo de Berardinis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IDEA2是一个将大语言模型(LLM)集成到协作式人机交互流程中的半自动化竞聘问题(CQ)提取框架，通过迭代循环机制让LLM提取、领域专家反馈、LLM重塑三个阶段形成闭环，并使用溯源模型追踪每个CQ的完整生命周期。该方法在科学数据和文化遗产两个真实场景中得到验证，证明能加速需求工程、提升CQ接受度和相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞聘问题(CQ)提取是本体工程中的关键但资源密集型瓶颈，现有方法面临领域专家（拥有知识）与本体工程师（形式化知识）之间的沟通鸿沟，导致需求工程效率低下且CQ质量难以保证。

Method: IDEA2采用迭代式半自动化工作流：(1)LLM从需求文档中初始提取CQs；(2)领域专家通过协作平台进行共同评审和反馈；(3)LLM根据反馈迭代重塑被拒绝的CQs直至达成共识。同时引入溯源模型完整记录每个CQ的编辑历史、匿名反馈和生成参数，确保透明性和可复现性。

Result: 在科学数据和文化遗产两个真实场景验证表明：IDEA2能加速需求工程进程，提升CQ的接受度和相关性，领域专家报告高可用性和有效性。

Conclusion: LLM与专家-in-the-loop范式的结合可有效弥合领域专家与本体工程师之间的沟通鸿沟，半自动化流程在保证专家主导的同时显著提高效率，溯源机制增强了方法论的可审计性和科学严谨性。

Abstract: Competency question (CQ) elicitation represents a critical but resource-intensive bottleneck in ontology engineering. This foundational phase is often hampered by the communication gap between domain experts, who possess the necessary knowledge, and ontology engineers, who formalise it. This paper introduces IDEA2, a novel, semi-automated workflow that integrates Large Language Models (LLMs) within a collaborative, expert-in-the-loop process to address this challenge. The methodology is characterised by a core iterative loop: an initial LLM-based extraction of CQs from requirement documents, a co-creational review and feedback phase by domain experts on an accessible collaborative platform, and an iterative, feedback-driven reformulation of rejected CQs by an LLM until consensus is achieved. To ensure transparency and reproducibility, the entire lifecycle of each CQ is tracked using a provenance model that captures the full lineage of edits, anonymised feedback, and generation parameters. The workflow was validated in 2 real-world scenarios (scientific data, cultural heritage), demonstrating that IDEA2 can accelerate the requirements engineering process, improve the acceptance and relevance of the resulting CQs, and exhibit high usability and effectiveness among domain experts. We release all code and experiments at https://github.com/KE-UniLiv/IDEA2

</details>


### [64] [A Role-Based LLM Framework for Structured Information Extraction from Healthy Food Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01529)
*Congjing Zhang,Ruoxuan Bao,Jingyu Li,Yoav Ackerman,Shuai Huang,Yanfang Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对健康食品政策信息抽取中的幻觉、错误分类和遗漏问题，提出基于角色的LLM框架，通过政策分析师、法律策略专家和食品系统专家三个专门角色，结合结构化领域知识，显著提升复杂推理任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在健康食品政策信息抽取中面临幻觉、错误分类和遗漏等挑战，这些问题源于政策文档的结构多样性和不一致性，限制了现有方法在实际应用中的可靠性。

Method: 提出基于角色的LLM框架，包含三个专门角色：政策分析师（处理元数据和机制分类）、法律策略专家（识别复杂法律方法）、食品系统专家（分类食品系统阶段）。通过将结构化领域知识整合到特定角色提示中模拟专家分析工作流程。

Result: 在608份HFPP数据库政策上评估，使用Llama-3.3-70B模型，与零样本、少样本和思维链基线对比，框架在复杂推理任务中表现最优。

Conclusion: 基于角色的LLM框架结合结构化领域知识，为健康食品政策信息抽取提供了可靠、透明且有效的自动化方法。

Abstract: Current Large Language Model (LLM) approaches for information extraction (IE) in the healthy food policy domain are often hindered by various factors, including misinformation, specifically hallucinations, misclassifications, and omissions that result from the structural diversity and inconsistency of policy documents. To address these limitations, this study proposes a role-based LLM framework that automates the IE from unstructured policy data by assigning specialized roles: an LLM policy analyst for metadata and mechanism classification, an LLM legal strategy specialist for identifying complex legal approaches, and an LLM food system expert for categorizing food system stages. This framework mimics expert analysis workflows by incorporating structured domain knowledge, including explicit definitions of legal mechanisms and classification criteria, into role-specific prompts. We evaluate the framework using 608 healthy food policies from the Healthy Food Policy Project (HFPP) database, comparing its performance against zero-shot, few-shot, and chain-of-thought (CoT) baselines using Llama-3.3-70B. Our proposed framework demonstrates superior performance in complex reasoning tasks, offering a reliable and transparent methodology for automating IE from health policies.

</details>


### [65] [Semantic Modeling for World-Centered Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01359)
*Andrei Mantsivoda,Darya Gavrilina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出世界中心多智能体系统（WMAS）作为传统智能体中心架构的替代方案，强调在企业等结构化领域需要共享的显式世界表示以确保语义一致性、可解释性和长期稳定性。核心贡献包括：世界分类维度、语义模型数学形式化，以及Ontobox平台实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能体中心架构在结构化领域（如企业、机构系统）中存在语义一致性不足、缺乏可解释性、长期稳定性欠佳等问题。现有架构中各智能体持有独立本地表示，难以保证全局一致性和系统行为的可验证性。

Method: 提出世界中心多智能体系统框架：（1）沿多个维度对"世界"进行分类（本体显性程度、规范性等）；（2）采用"语义模型"作为世界的数学形式化表示；（3）学习和协调过程基于共享世界模型而非孤立智能体本地表示。

Result: WMAS框架实现了全局一致性、语义一致性保证和可验证的系统行为。智能体通过共享世界模型进行交互，而非依赖各自分散的内部表示。

Conclusion: 语义模型为表示复杂组织世界提供了充分的数学基础，Ontobox平台验证了WMAS架构的可行性。该研究为多智能体系统在企业级和机构级应用中的发展提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: We introduce world-centered multi-agent systems (WMAS) as an alternative to traditional agent-centered architectures, arguing that structured domains such as enterprises and institutional systems require a shared, explicit world representation to ensure semantic consistency, explainability, and long-term stability. We classify worlds along dimensions including ontological explicitness, normativity, etc. In WMAS, learning and coordination operate over a shared world model rather than isolated agent-local representations, enabling global consistency and verifiable system behavior. We propose semantic models as a mathematical formalism for representing such worlds. Finally, we present the Ontobox platform as a realization of WMAS.

</details>

Abstract: In this work, we extend our systematic capacity region perspective to multi-junction traffic networks, focussing on the special case of an urban corridor network. ### [67] [Crashing Waves vs. Rising Tides: Preliminary Findings on AI Automation from Thousands of Worker Evaluations of Labor Market Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01363)
*Matthias Mertens,Adam Kuzee,Brittany S. Harris,Harry Lyu,Wensu Li,Jonathan Rosenfeld,Meiri Anto,Martin Fleming,Neil Thompson*

Abstract: We propose that AI automation is a continuum between: (i) crashing waves where AI capabilities surge abruptly over small sets of tasks, and (ii) rising tides where the increase in AI capabilities is more continuous and broad-based. We test for these effects in preliminary evidence from an ongoing evaluation of AI capabilities across over 3,000 broad-based tasks derived from the U.S. Department of Labor O*NET categorization that are text-based and thus LLM-addressable. Based on more than 17,000 evaluations by workers from these jobs, we find little evidence of crashing waves (in contrast to recent work by METR), but substantial evidence that rising tides are the primary form of AI automation. AI performance is high and improving rapidly across a wide range of tasks. We estimate that, in 2024-Q2, AI models successfully complete tasks that take humans approximately 3-4 hours with about a 50% success rate, increasing to about 65% by 2025-Q3. If recent trends in AI capability growth persist, this pace of AI improvement implies that LLMs will be able to complete most text-related tasks with success rates of, on average, 80%-95% by 2029 at a minimally sufficient quality level. Achieving near-perfect success rates at this quality level or comparable success rates at superior quality would require several additional years. These AI capability improvements would impact the economy and labor market as organizations adopt AI, which could have a substantially longer timeline.

</details>


### [68] [CogBias: Measuring and Mitigating Cognitive Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01366)
*Fan Huang,Songheng Zhang,Haewoon Kwak,Jisun An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM认知偏见基准LLM CogBias，涵盖判断、信息处理、社会和响应四类偏见族。研究发现：提示级去偏见对响应偏见有效但对判断偏见适得其反；认知偏见在模型激活空间中以线性可分方向编码；激活 steering 可降低26-32%偏见评分且保持下游能力；不同架构模型尽管偏见表征近乎正交（平均余弦相似度0.01），但 steering 效果相似，揭示了跨架构的共享功能组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被部署于高风险决策场景，但之前研究仅证明LLM存在行为层面的认知偏见，缺乏可量化的ground-truth基准，且未探索偏见是否对应可识别的内部表征以及能否通过定向干预缓解。

Method: 1) 提出LLM认知偏见的形式化定义并构建LLM CogBias基准（含四类偏见族）；2) 评估三种LLM在各类偏见上的表现；3) 采用对比设计下的线性探针分析激活空间表征；4) 应用激活steering调节偏见行为；5) 在25个下游基准上测试能力保持。

Result: 认知偏见在所有四类家族中系统性出现，幅度和去偏见响应呈家族依赖性；提示级去偏见显著减少响应偏见但对判断偏见适得其反；偏见在激活空间中编码为线性可分方向；激活steering在保持下游能力的同时降低26-32%偏见评分；尽管跨模型偏见表征近乎正交（平均余弦相似度0.01），steering在不同架构上以相似速率减少偏见。

Conclusion: 认知偏见在LLM中具有可识别的神经表征基础，且可通过激活干预进行调节；跨模型近正交表征但相似干预效果的现象，揭示了不同LLM架构间存在共享的功能组织结构，为通用去偏见策略提供了可能性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes decision-making contexts. While prior work has shown that LLMs exhibit cognitive biases behaviorally, whether these biases correspond to identifiable internal representations and can be mitigated through targeted intervention remains an open question. We define LLM cognitive bias as systematic, reproducible deviations from correct answers in tasks with computable ground-truth baselines, and introduce LLM CogBias, a benchmark organized around four families of cognitive biases: Judgment, Information Processing, Social, and Response. We evaluate three LLMs and find that cognitive biases emerge systematically across all four families, with magnitudes and debiasing responses that are strongly family-dependent: prompt-level debiasing substantially reduces Response biases but backfires for Judgment biases. Using linear probes under a contrastive design, we show that these biases are encoded as linearly separable directions in model activation space. Finally, we apply activation steering to modulate biased behavior, achieving 26--32\% reduction in bias score (fraction of biased responses) while preserving downstream capability on 25 benchmarks (Llama: negligible degradation; Qwen: up to $-$19.0pp for Judgment biases). Despite near-orthogonal bias representations across models (mean cosine similarity 0.01), steering reduces bias at similar rates across architectures ($r(246)$=.621, $p$<.001), suggesting shared functional organization.

</details>

Abstract: Agentic AI shifts the investor's role from analytical execution to oversight. We present an agentic strategic asset allocation pipeline in which approximately 50 specialized agents produce capital market assumptions, construct portfolios using over 20 competing methods, and critique and vote on each other's output. A researcher agent proposes new portfolio construction methods not yet represented, and a meta-agent compares past forecasts against realized returns and rewrites agent code and prompts to improve future performance. The entire pipeline is governed by the Investment Policy Statement--the same document that guides human portfolio managers can now constrain and direct autonomous agents.

### [70] [RIFT: A RubrIc Failure Mode Taxonomy and Automated Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01375)
*Zhengyang Qi,Charles Dickens,Derek Pham,Amanda Dsouza,Armin Parchami,Frederic Sala,Paroma Varma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RIFT是一个用于诊断LLM评估中rubric质量问题的失效模式分类体系，包含8种失效模式（分为可靠性、内容效度和结果效度三大类），通过5个跨领域基准的归纳式构建，并提供了可扩展的自动化诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM基准测试和训练流程中广泛使用基于评分标准（rubric）的评估方法，但现有方法只能通过下游信号（如强化学习效果）间接验证rubric有效性，缺乏对rubric本身质量问题的系统诊断方法。

Method: 采用扎根理论（grounded theory）方法，从5个跨领域基准（涵盖通用指令遵循、代码生成、创意写作、专家级深度研究）中迭代抽取rubric进行标注，直至未发现新的失效模式。同时提出自动化rubric质量指标以支持可扩展诊断。

Result: 1）分类体系包含8种失效模式，分为三类：可靠性失效（Reliability Failures）、内容效度失效（Content Validity Failures）、结果效度失效（Consequential Validity Failures）；2）标注一致性达87%成对一致率和0.64平均Cohen's kappa；3）自动化指标与人工标注对齐，F1达0.86。

Conclusion: RIFT为rubric评估质量提供了系统化的诊断框架，其自动化指标可有效辅助大规模rubric质量评估，为LLM训练和评估中的评分标准设计提供了理论与工具支撑。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is widely used in LLM benchmarks and training pipelines for open-ended, less verifiable tasks. While prior work has demonstrated the effectiveness of rubrics using downstream signals such as reinforcement learning outcomes, there remains no principled way to diagnose rubric quality issues from such aggregated or downstream signals alone. To address this gap, we introduce RIFT: RubrIc Failure mode Taxonomy, a taxonomy for systematically characterizing failure modes in rubric composition and design. RIFT consists of eight failure modes organized into three high-level categories: Reliability Failures, Content Validity Failures, and Consequential Validity Failures. RIFT is developed using grounded theory by iteratively annotating rubrics drawn from five diverse benchmarks spanning general instruction following, code generation, creative writing, and expert-level deep research, until no new failure modes are identified. We evaluate the consistency of the taxonomy by measuring agreement among independent human annotators, observing fair agreement overall (87% pairwise agreement and 0.64 average Cohen's kappa). Finally, to support scalable diagnosis, we propose automated rubric quality metrics and show that they align with human failure-mode annotations, achieving up to 0.86 F1.

</details>


### [71] [Leveraging the Value of Information in POMDP Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01434)
*Zakariya Laouar,Qi Heng Ho,Zachary Sunberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用价值信息（VOI）在信念空间中变化结构的POMDP求解框架VOIMCP，通过选择性忽略低VOI观测信息来减少不必要的观测分支，从而在有限规划时间内获得更高效的性能策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POMDP为不确定性下的规划问题提供了原则性形式化，但受制于维度灾难和历史灾难，在有限规划时间内获得高性能策略仍是主要挑战。

Method: 引入动态规划框架利用VOI在信念空间中的变化结构；提出VOIMCP算法，通过条件性处理观测信息，在VOI较低时选择性忽略观测以避免不必要的观测分支。

Result: VOIMCP在多个POMDP基准测试中优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 提供了VOI推理框架的近最优性理论保证，并推导了VOIMCP的非渐近收敛边界。

Abstract: Partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) offer a principled formalism for planning under state and transition uncertainty. Despite advances made towards solving large POMDPs, obtaining performant policies under limited planning time remains a major challenge due to the curse of dimensionality and the curse of history. For many POMDP problems, the value of information (VOI) - the expected performance gain from reasoning about observations - varies over the belief space. We introduce a dynamic programming framework that exploits this structure by conditionally processing observations based on the value of information at each belief. Building on this framework, we propose Value of Information Monte Carlo planning (VOIMCP), a Monte Carlo Tree Search algorithm that allocates computational effort more efficiently by selectively disregarding observation information when the VOI is low, avoiding unnecessary branching of observations. We provide theoretical guarantees on the near-optimality of our VOI reasoning framework and derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds for VOIMCP. Simulation evaluations demonstrate that VOIMCP outperforms baselines on several POMDP benchmarks.

</details>


### [72] [ClawSafety: "Safe" LLMs, Unsafe Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01438)
*Bowen Wei,Yunbei Zhang,Jinhao Pan,Kai Mei,Xiao Wang,Jihun Hamm,Ziwei Zhu,Yingqiang Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLAWSAFETY is a benchmark of 120 adversarial scenarios testing prompt injection attacks on AI agents running with elevated privileges. Evaluations of 5 LLMs across 2,520 trials reveal 40-75% attack success rates, with safety depending jointly on model capability and agent framework design.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Personal AI agents run with elevated privileges on local machines, making single prompt injection attacks capable of leaking credentials, redirecting financial transactions, or destroying files. Existing safety evaluations test models in isolated settings with synthetic environments, failing to account for how agent frameworks shape safety outcomes.

Method: Introduced CLAWSAFETY benchmark with 120 adversarial test scenarios organized along three dimensions (harm domain, attack vector, harmful action type), grounded in realistic high-privilege professional workspaces (software engineering, finance, healthcare, law, DevOps). Adversarial content embedded in three channels: workspace skill files, trusted emails, and web pages. Evaluated 5 frontier LLMs across 2,520 sandboxed trials, including cross-scaffold experiments on 3 agent frameworks.

Result: Attack success rates ranged 40-75% across models, varying sharply by injection vector. Skill instructions (highest trust) consistently more dangerous than email or web content. Strongest model maintained hard boundaries against credential forwarding and destructive actions; weaker models permitted both. Safety is not determined by backbone model alone but depends on the full deployment stack.

Conclusion: Safety evaluation must treat model and framework as joint variables. The agent framework itself significantly shapes safety outcomes, calling for comprehensive evaluation approaches that account for the full deployment stack rather than model capability in isolation.

Abstract: Personal AI agents like OpenClaw run with elevated privileges on users' local machines, where a single successful prompt injection can leak credentials, redirect financial transactions, or destroy files. This threat goes well beyond conventional text-level jailbreaks, yet existing safety evaluations fall short: most test models in isolated chat settings, rely on synthetic environments, and do not account for how the agent framework itself shapes safety outcomes. We introduce CLAWSAFETY, a benchmark of 120 adversarial test scenarios organized along three dimensions (harm domain, attack vector, and harmful action type) and grounded in realistic, high-privilege professional workspaces spanning software engineering, finance, healthcare, law, and DevOps. Each test case embeds adversarial content in one of three channels the agent encounters during normal work: workspace skill files, emails from trusted senders, and web pages. We evaluate five frontier LLMs as agent backbones, running 2,520 sandboxed trials across all configurations. Attack success rates (ASR) range from 40\% to 75\% across models and vary sharply by injection vector, with skill instructions (highest trust) consistently more dangerous than email or web content. Action-trace analysis reveals that the strongest model maintains hard boundaries against credential forwarding and destructive actions, while weaker models permit both. Cross-scaffold experiments on three agent frameworks further demonstrate that safety is not determined by the backbone model alone but depends on the full deployment stack, calling for safety evaluation that treats model and framework as joint variables.

</details>


### [73] [When AI Gets it Wong: Reliability and Risk in AI-Assisted Medication Decision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01449)
*Khalid Adnan Alsayed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文聚焦AI辅助药物系统的可靠性问题，通过模拟场景分析系统失效类型及其潜在临床后果，强调传统性能指标不足以评估安全关键领域的真实风险，呼吁建立风险感知型评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI药物系统在标准评估中表现良好，但其在真实临床决策中的可靠性尚不清楚。在药物管理等高风险领域，单次错误推荐即可导致严重患者伤害，因此亟需深入理解系统失效行为及其实际影响。

Method: 采用受控模拟场景方法，模拟药物相互作用和剂量决策情境，系统性地分析不同类型的系统失效模式，包括：遗漏药物相互作用、错误风险标记、不当剂量推荐。

Result: 研究发现AI在药物相关场景中的错误可导致：药物不良反应、治疗无效或护理延误。过度依赖AI推荐且缺乏充分人工监督时会放大这些风险。此外，决策过程透明度不足进一步加剧了系统可靠性挑战。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了以可靠性为核心的AI评估视角，强调需将风险感知型评估方法纳入传统性能指标体系，尤其在药房实践等安全关键领域。建议未来研究超越聚合指标，聚焦失效行为模式和真实世界影响。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly integrated into healthcare and pharmacy workflows, supporting tasks such as medication recommendations, dosage determination, and drug interaction detection. While these systems often demonstrate strong performance under standard evaluation metrics, their reliability in real-world decision-making remains insufficiently understood. In high-risk domains such as medication management, even a single incorrect recommendation can result in severe patient harm. This paper examines the reliability of AI-assisted medication systems by focusing on system failures and their potential clinical consequences. Rather than evaluating performance solely through aggregate metrics, this work shifts attention towards how errors occur and what happens when AI systems produce incorrect outputs. Through a series of controlled, simulated scenarios involving drug interactions and dosage decisions, we analyse different types of system failures, including missed interactions, incorrect risk flagging, and inappropriate dosage recommendations. The findings highlight that AI errors in medication-related contexts can lead to adverse drug reactions, ineffective treatment, or delayed care, particularly when systems are used without sufficient human oversight. Furthermore, the paper discusses the risks of over-reliance on AI recommendations and the challenges posed by limited transparency in decision-making processes. This work contributes a reliability-focused perspective on AI evaluation in healthcare, emphasising the importance of understanding failure behavior and real-world impact. It highlights the need to complement traditional performance metrics with risk-aware evaluation approaches, particularly in safety-critical domains such as pharmacy practice.

</details>


### [74] [A Multi-Agent Human-LLM Collaborative Framework for Closed-Loop Scientific Literature Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01452)
*Maxwell J. Jacobson,Daniel Xie,Jackson Shen,Adil Wazeer,Haiyan Wang,Xinghang Zhang,Yexiang Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Elhuyar是一个多智能体、人在回路的AI系统，用于从科学文献中提取、分析和迭代优化洞察。该系统整合了大语言模型、结构化AI和人类科学家的协作，通过专业化智能体分工完成论文筛选、数据提取、模型拟合和结果总结，并在材料科学领域（钨在氦离子辐照下的研究）得到验证，成功发现了氦泡生长与辐照剂量和温度的指数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现受限于碎片化的文献环境，人类在收集、分析和理解科学文献方面需要投入大量努力。现有AI工具（包括自动摘要和问答系统）缺乏从科学文献中提取深度洞察所需的结构化、多步骤方法。虽然大语言模型（LLM）为文献分析提供了新可能，但由于幻觉和提取不完整等问题，其可靠性仍然不足。

Method: Elhuyar采用多智能体架构，各专业智能体分工协作完成任务：筛选论文、提取数据、拟合模型和总结发现，并通过人类监督确保可靠性。该系统集成了LLM、结构化AI和人类科学家三种能力，支持迭代优化的工作流程。

Result: 在材料科学领域的应用中，Elhuyar分析了钨在氦离子辐照条件下的文献，发现氦泡生长与辐照剂量和温度呈指数相关性（实验证实的规律），为聚变反应堆中等离子体面向材料（PFM）的设计提供了重要参考。

Conclusion: Elhuyar展示了AI辅助文献综述如何发现科学规律并加速科学发现。该框架通过整合多智能体系统、结构化AI和人类专业知识，有效解决了科学文献分析中的碎片化和可靠性问题，为跨领域的AI驱动科学研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is slowed by fragmented literature that requires excessive human effort to gather, analyze, and understand. AI tools, including autonomous summarization and question answering, have been developed to aid in understanding scientific literature. However, these tools lack the structured, multi-step approach necessary for extracting deep insights from scientific literature. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new possibilities for literature analysis, but remain unreliable due to hallucinations and incomplete extraction. We introduce Elhuyar, a multi-agent, human-in-the-loop system that integrates LLMs, structured AI, and human scientists to extract, analyze, and iteratively refine insights from scientific literature. The framework distributes tasks among specialized agents for filtering papers, extracting data, fitting models, and summarizing findings, with human oversight ensuring reliability. The system generates structured reports with extracted data, visualizations, model equations, and text summaries, enabling deeper inquiry through iterative refinement. Deployed in materials science, it analyzed literature on tungsten under helium-ion irradiation, showing experimentally correlated exponential helium bubble growth with irradiation dose and temperature, offering insight for plasma-facing materials (PFMs) in fusion reactors. This demonstrates how AI-assisted literature review can uncover scientific patterns and accelerate discovery.

</details>


### [75] [Infeasibility Aware Large Language Models for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01455)
*Yakun Wang,Min Chen,Zeguan Wu,Junyu Liu,Sitao Zhang,Zhenwen Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向NP-hard组合优化问题的"不可行性感知"框架，通过可验证数据集构建、监督微调和LLM引导的下游搜索相结合，使8B参数LLM能够联合执行解生成与不可行性检测，并在minor-embedding问题上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多聚焦于可行解生成，缺乏对不可行实例的显式检测能力，而NP-hard问题的不可行性验证对于实际应用至关重要。

Method: 1) 提出minor-embedding问题的新数学规划 formulation 及可证明的零相位不可行性筛选方法，实现大规模带标签训练实例构建（标注为"可行带结构化证书"或"可证明不可行"）；2) 基于该精确优化流水线生成训练数据进行监督微调；3) 将微调LLM输出作为下游局部搜索的热启动。

Result: 微调模型总体准确率较GPT-5.2提升最高30%；LLM引导的热启动使下游局部搜索相比从零开始加速最高2倍。

Conclusion: 通过将不可行性检测整合到LLM训练流程，并利用LLM输出热启动下游优化，可在保证解质量的同时显著提升效率，为LLM辅助求解NP-hard问题提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly explored for NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems, but most existing methods emphasize feasible-instance solution generation and do not explicitly address infeasibility detection. We propose an infeasibility-aware framework that combines certifiable dataset construction, supervised fine-tuning, and LLM-assisted downstream search. For the minor-embedding problem, we introduce a new mathematical programming formulation together with provable zero-phase infeasibility screening, which enables scalable construction of training instances labeled either as feasible with structured certificates or as certifiably infeasible. Using training data generated through this exact optimization pipeline, we show that an 8B-parameter LLM can be fine-tuned to jointly perform solution generation and infeasibility detection. We further utilize LLM outputs as warm starts for downstream local search, providing a practical way to accelerate optimization even when the LLM outputs are imperfect. Experiments show that our fine-tuned model improves overall accuracy by up to 30\% over GPT-5.2; meanwhile LLM-guided warm starts provide up to $2\times$ speedup compared with starting from scratch in downstream local search.

</details>


### [76] [Reducing Hallucinations in LLM-based Scientific Literature Analysis Using Peer Context Outlier Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01461)
*Daniel Xie,Maxwell J. Jacobson,Adil Wazeer,Haiyan Wang,Xinghang Zhang,Yexiang Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: P-COD通过利用语料库中文档之间的关系来减少LLM幻觉，通过同行验证机制调整置信度评分，在6个科学领域实现高达98%的异常检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM数据提取方法（提示工程、思维链提示）仅关注单个文档，忽视了语料库中文档间的关联性，导致幻觉问题无法系统性解决。在科学文献摘要领域，具有相似实验设置的论文应得出相似结论，这一特性可用于验证提取结果的可靠性。

Method: 提出Peer Context Outlier Detection (P-COD)方法：1) 对语料库中的文档进行聚类/关联分析；2) 将提取数据与同行验证信息进行比对；3) 调整置信度评分并标记低置信度结果供专家审核；4) 高置信度结果（同行支持）视为可靠。

Result: 在6个科学领域的实验中，异常检测精度达到98%，有效减少幻觉、增强自动化系统可信度、简化数据提取流程。

Conclusion: 基于文档关系的验证机制是减少LLM幻觉的有效途径，同行验证策略可显著提升科学文献数据提取的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Reducing hallucinations in Large Language Models (LLMs) is essential for improving the accuracy of data extraction from large text corpora. Current methods, like prompt engineering and chain-of-thought prompting, focus on individual documents but fail to consider relationships across a corpus. This paper introduces Peer Context Outlier Detection (P-COD), a novel approach that uses the relationships between documents to improve extraction accuracy. Our application domain is in scientific literature summarization, where papers with similar experiment settings should draw similar conclusions. By comparing extracted data to validated peer information within the corpus, we adjust confidence scores and flag low-confidence results for expert review. High-confidence results, supported by peer validation, are considered reliable. Our experiments demonstrate up to 98% precision in outlier detection across 6 domains of science, demonstrating that our design reduces hallucinations, enhances trust in automated systems, and allows researchers to focus on ambiguous cases, streamlining the data extraction workflows.

</details>


### [77] [A Self-Evolving Agentic Framework for Metasurface Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01480)
*Yi Huang,Bowen Zheng,Yunxi Dong,Hong Tang,Huan Zhao,S. M. Rakibul Hasan Shawon,Hualiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于智能体框架的介电面（metasurface）逆向设计方法，通过上下文级技能演化解决现有语言驱动系统在逆向设计任务中缺乏可重用工作流知识的问题。该框架将编程智能体、演化技能工件与基于物理模拟的确定性评估器相结合，在无需修改模型权重或底层物理求解器的情况下迭代优化求解器特定策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介电面逆向设计虽已成为实现复杂光学功能的核心技术，但将目标响应转化为可执行的工作流仍需计算电磁学和求解器特定软件工程方面的专业知识。现有大语言模型虽提供了降低工作流构建负担的替代路径，但仍受限于会话边界，无法跨逆向设计任务保留可重用的工作流知识。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 编程智能体，负责生成和修改代码；(2) 演化技能工件，存储跨任务累积的求解器特定策略；(3) 基于物理模拟的确定性评估器。框架通过迭代优化使求解器特定策略在任务间不断精炼，而无需修改模型权重或底层物理求解器。

Result: 在包含多个介电面逆向设计任务类型的基准测试中，演化技能将使分布内任务成功率从38%提升至74%，标准通过率从0.510提升至0.870，平均尝试次数从4.10次降至2.30次。在保留任务族上，二元成功率仅有边际变化，但最佳边界改进、错误构成转变和智能体行为变化表明工作流知识存在部分迁移。

Conclusion: 技能演化的主要价值在于围绕可靠计算引擎积累可重用的求解器特定专业知识，为实现更自主、更易访问的介电面逆向设计工作流提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Metasurface inverse design has become central to realizing complex optical functionality, yet translating target responses into executable, solver-compatible workflows still demands specialized expertise in computational electromagnetics and solver-specific software engineering. Recent large language models (LLMs) offer a complementary route to reducing this workflow-construction burden, but existing language-driven systems remain largely session-bounded and do not preserve reusable workflow knowledge across inverse-design tasks. We present an agentic framework for metasurface inverse design that addresses this limitation through context-level skill evolution. The framework couples a coding agent, evolving skill artifacts, and a deterministic evaluator grounded in physical simulation so that solver-specific strategies can be iteratively refined across tasks without modifying model weights or the underlying physics solver. We evaluate the framework on a benchmark spanning multiple metasurface inverse-design task types, with separate training-aligned and held-out task families. Evolved skills raise in-distribution task success from 38% to 74%, increase criteria pass fraction from 0.510 to 0.870, and reduce average attempts from 4.10 to 2.30. On held-out task families, binary success changes only marginally, but improvements in best margin together with shifts in error composition and agent behavior indicate partial transfer of workflow knowledge. These results suggest that the main value of skill evolution lies in accumulating reusable solver-specific expertise around reliable computational engines, thereby offering a practical path toward more autonomous and accessible metasurface inverse-design workflows.

</details>


### [78] [PHMForge: A Scenario-Driven Agentic Benchmark for Industrial Asset Lifecycle Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01532)
*Ayan Das,Dhaval Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PHMForge是首个针对LLM智能体在工业预测与健康管理（PHM）任务中的综合评估基准，通过75个专家策划的场景和65个专业工具评估主流框架（如ReAct、Cursor Agent、Claude Code）在5类核心任务上的表现。研究发现即使最优配置也仅达68%任务完成率，暴露出工具编排错误（23%）、多资产推理缺陷及跨设备泛化能力不足等系统性弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体基准测试缺乏对工业安全与金融风险敏感场景的严格评估能力，现有的通用基准无法反映真实工业环境中的复杂工具编排需求，存在关键的评估空白。
